<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns:th="http://www.thymeleaf.org">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>Ajout de Plats & Recettes</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap-icons@1.10.0/font/bootstrap-icons.css">
</head>
<body class="bg-light">
<div class="container my-5">
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <h2>Saisie Multiple de Plats & Recettes</h2>
        <a th:href="@{/plats}" class="btn btn-secondary">Retour à la liste</a>
    </div>

    <form th:action="@{/plats/save-multiple}" method="post" id="platsForm">
        <div id="platsContainer">
            <!-- Bloc Dynamique Plat N°0 par défaut -->
            <div class="card mb-4 plat-block border-primary shadow-sm" data-plat-index="0">
                <div class="card-header bg-primary text-white d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                    <h5 class="mb-0">Plat #<span class="plat-number">1</span></h5>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger btn-remove-plat" style="display: none;">
                        <i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i> Supprimer ce plat
                    </button>
                </div>
                <div class="card-body">
                    <div class="row g-3 mb-3">
                        <div class="col-md-5">
                            <label class="form-label">Nom du Plat *</label>
                            <input type="text" name="plats[0].nom" class="form-control" required placeholder="ex: Pizza Margherita">
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-md-4">
                            <label class="form-label">Catégorie *</label>
                            <select name="plats[0].idCategorie" class="form-select" required>
                                <option value="">-- Sélectionner --</option>
                                <option th:each="c : ${categories}" th:value="${c.id}" th:text="${c.libelle}"></option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-md-3">
                            <label class="form-label">Prix de Vente (Ar) *</label>
                            <input type="number" step="0.01" name="plats[0].prixVente" class="form-control" required placeholder="0.00">
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <h6 class="mt-4 text-secondary">Composition de la Recette (Ingrédients)</h6>
                    <div class="ingredients-container" data-plat-index="0">
                        <!-- Ligne d'ingrédient initiale -->
                        <div class="row g-2 mb-2 ingredient-row align-items-center">
                            <div class="col-md-6">
                                <select name="plats[0].ingredients[0].idIngredient" class="form-select">
                                    <option value="">-- Choisir un Ingrédient --</option>
                                    <option th:each="ing : ${allIngredients}" th:value="${ing.id}" 
                                            th:text="${ing.nom + ' (' + ing.unite.symbole + ')'}"></option>
                                </select>
                            </div>
                            <div class="col-md-4">
                                <input type="number" step="0.001" name="plats[0].ingredients[0].quantiteRequise" class="form-control" placeholder="Quantité requise">
                            </div>
                            <div class="col-md-2">
                                <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline-danger w-100 btn-remove-ing">
                                    <i class="bi bi-trash"></i>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-success mt-2 btn-add-ing">
                        <i class="bi bi-plus-circle"></i> Ajouter un Ingrédient
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="d-flex gap-2 justify-content-between mb-5">
            <button type="button" id="btnAddPlat" class="btn btn-info text-white">
                <i class="bi bi-plus-square"></i> Ajouter un autre Plat
            </button>
            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-success btn-lg">
                <i class="bi bi-check-circle"></i> Enregistrer tous les plats
            </button>
        </div>
    </form>
</div>

<!-- Template HTML caché pour les Ingrédients -->
<template id="ingredientTemplate">
    <div class="row g-2 mb-2 ingredient-row align-items-center">
        <div class="col-md-6">
            <select class="form-select ing-select">
                <option value="">-- Choisir un Ingrédient --</option>
                <option th:each="ing : ${allIngredients}" th:value="${ing.id}" 
                        th:text="${ing.nom + ' (' + (ing.unite != null ? ing.unite.symbole : '') + ')'}"></option>
            </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
            <input type="number" step="0.001" class="form-control ing-qty" placeholder="Quantité requise">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-2">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline-danger w-100 btn-remove-ing">
                <i class="bi bi-trash"></i>
            </button>
        </div>
    </div>
</template>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    let platIndex = 0;

    // Ajouter un nouveau Plat
    document.getElementById("btnAddPlat").addEventListener("click", function () {
        platIndex++;
        const container = document.getElementById("platsContainer");
        const firstBlock = container.querySelector(".plat-block");
        const newBlock = firstBlock.cloneNode(true);

        newBlock.setAttribute("data-plat-index", platIndex);
        newBlock.querySelector(".plat-number").textContent = platIndex + 1;

        // Réinitialiser les champs textes/selects du plat
        newBlock.querySelector("input[name*='.nom']").name = `plats[${platIndex}].nom`;
        newBlock.querySelector("input[name*='.nom']").value = "";
        
        newBlock.querySelector("select[name*='.idCategorie']").name = `plats[${platIndex}].idCategorie`;
        newBlock.querySelector("select[name*='.idCategorie']").selectedIndex = 0;
        
        newBlock.querySelector("input[name*='.prixVente']").name = `plats[${platIndex}].prixVente`;
        newBlock.querySelector("input[name*='.prixVente']").value = "";

        // Nettoyer les ingrédients pour n'en garder qu'un seul vierge
        const ingContainer = newBlock.querySelector(".ingredients-container");
        ingContainer.setAttribute("data-plat-index", platIndex);
        ingContainer.innerHTML = "";

        // Ajouter un premier ingrédient vide
        appendIngredientRow(ingContainer, platIndex, 0);

        // Afficher le bouton supprimer plat
        const deleteBtn = newBlock.querySelector(".btn-remove-plat");
        deleteBtn.style.display = "block";

        container.appendChild(newBlock);
        updatePlatIndexes();
    });

    // Gestion des événements délégués (Boutons dynamiques)
    document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        // Ajouter Ingrédient
        if (e.target.closest(".btn-add-ing")) {
            const platBlock = e.target.closest(".plat-block");
            const pIdx = platBlock.getAttribute("data-plat-index");
            const ingContainer = platBlock.querySelector(".ingredients-container");
            const ingRows = ingContainer.querySelectorAll(".ingredient-row");
            appendIngredientRow(ingContainer, pIdx, ingRows.length);
        }

        // Supprimer Ingrédient
        if (e.target.closest(".btn-remove-ing")) {
            const row = e.target.closest(".ingredient-row");
            const container = row.closest(".ingredients-container");
            if (container.querySelectorAll(".ingredient-row").length > 1) {
                row.remove();
                reindexIngredients(container);
            } else {
                alert("Un plat doit avoir au moins une ligne d'ingrédient ou être laissée vide.");
            }
        }

        // Supprimer Plat
        if (e.target.closest(".btn-remove-plat")) {
            const block = e.target.closest(".plat-block");
            block.remove();
            updatePlatIndexes();
        }
    });

    function appendIngredientRow(container, pIdx, ingIdx) {
        const template = document.getElementById("ingredientTemplate").content.cloneNode(true);
        const row = template.querySelector(".ingredient-row");
        
        const select = row.querySelector(".ing-select");
        select.name = `plats[${pIdx}].ingredients[${ingIdx}].idIngredient`;

        const input = row.querySelector(".ing-qty");
        input.name = `plats[${pIdx}].ingredients[${ingIdx}].quantiteRequise`;

        container.appendChild(row);
    }

    function reindexIngredients(container) {
        const pIdx = container.getAttribute("data-plat-index");
        const rows = container.querySelectorAll(".ingredient-row");
        rows.forEach((row, ingIdx) => {
            const select = row.querySelector("select");
            const input = row.querySelector("input");
            select.name = `plats[${pIdx}].ingredients[${ingIdx}].idIngredient`;
            input.name = `plats[${pIdx}].ingredients[${ingIdx}].quantiteRequise`;
        });
    }

    function updatePlatIndexes() {
        const blocks = document.querySelectorAll(".plat-block");
        blocks.forEach((block, index) => {
            block.setAttribute("data-plat-index", index);
            block.querySelector(".plat-number").textContent = index + 1;
            
            block.querySelector("input[name*='.nom']").name = `plats[${index}].nom`;
            block.querySelector("select[name*='.idCategorie']").name = `plats[${index}].idCategorie`;
            block.querySelector("input[name*='.prixVente']").name = `plats[${index}].prixVente`;
            
            const ingContainer = block.querySelector(".ingredients-container");
            ingContainer.setAttribute("data-plat-index", index);
            reindexIngredients(ingContainer);

            const deleteBtn = block.querySelector(".btn-remove-plat");
            deleteBtn.style.display = blocks.length > 1 ? "block" : "none";
        });
        platIndex = blocks.length - 1;
    }
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns:th="http://www.thymeleaf.org" lang="fr">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>Créer / Modifier une Recette</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css">

<div class="container my-5">
    <div class="card shadow">
        <div class="card-header bg-primary text-white d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
            <h3 class="mb-0">Composition de la Recette du Plat</h3>
            <a th:href="@{/plats}" class="btn btn-outline-light btn-sm">Retour à la liste</a>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
            
            <form th:action="@{/plats/recette/save}" method="post" id="recetteForm">
                <!-- Identification du Plat -->

                    <div class="col-md-6">
                        <label for="platId" class="form-label font-weight-bold">Sélectionner le Plat :</label>
                        <select class="form-select" id="platId" name="platId" required>
                            <option value="" disabled selected>-- Choisir un plat --</option>

                            </option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <hr>
                
                <!-- Section Ingrédients Dynamiques -->
                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
                    <h4 class="card-title text-secondary mb-0">Ingrédients Requis</h4>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-success" id="btnAddIngredient">
                        <i class="bi bi-plus-circle"></i> Ajouter un ingrédient
                    </button>
                </div>

                <div class="table-responsive">
                    <table class="table table-bordered align-middle" id="ingredientsTable">
                        <thead class="table-dark">
                            <tr>
                                <th style="width: 50%;">Ingrédient / Produit</th>
                                <th style="width: 35%;">Quantité Nécessaire</th>
                                <th style="width: 15%;" class="text-center">Action</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody id="ingredientsContainer">
                            <!-- Ligne par défaut (Index 0) -->
                            <tr class="ingredient-row">
                                <td>
                                    <select name="ingredients[0].idProduit" class="form-select produit-select" required>
                                        <option value="" disabled selected>-- Sélectionner l'ingrédient --</option>
                                        <option th:each="prod : ${produits}" 
                                                th:value="${prod.id}" 
                                                th:text="${prod.nom + ' (' + prod.uniteMesure + ')'}">
                                        </option>
                                    </select>
                                </td>
                                <td>
                                    <input type="number" step="0.01" min="0.01" name="ingredients[0].quantite" 
                                           class="form-control" placeholder="Ex: 0.500" required>
                                </td>
                                <td class="text-center">
                                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm remove-row" disabled>
                                        <i class="bi bi-trash"></i>
                                    </button>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <div class="d-grid gap-2 d-md-flex justify-content-md-end mt-4">
                    <button type="reset" class="btn btn-secondary me-md-2">Réinitialiser</button>
                    <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">Enregistrer la Recette</button>
                </div>
            </form>

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<!-- Dynamic Rows Script -->
<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    const container = document.getElementById('ingredientsContainer');
    const btnAdd = document.getElementById('btnAddIngredient');

    // Mettre à jour les indices des formulaires après chaque modification
    function updateRowIndices() {
        const rows = container.querySelectorAll('.ingredient-row');
        rows.forEach((row, index) => {
            const select = row.querySelector('.produit-select');
            const input = row.querySelector('input[type="number"]');

            select.name = `ingredients[${index}].idProduit`;
            input.name = `ingredients[${index}].quantite`;

            // Désactiver le bouton de suppression s'il n'y a qu'une seule ligne
            const btnRemove = row.querySelector('.remove-row');
            if (rows.length === 1) {
                btnRemove.setAttribute('disabled', 'true');
            } else {
                btnRemove.removeAttribute('disabled');
            }
        });
    }

    // Ajouter une nouvelle ligne
    btnAdd.addEventListener('click', function () {
        const firstRow = container.querySelector('.ingredient-row');
        const newRow = firstRow.cloneNode(true);

        // Réinitialiser les valeurs des inputs clonés
        newRow.querySelector('.produit-select').value = '';
        newRow.querySelector('input[type="number"]').value = '';

        container.appendChild(newRow);
        updateRowIndices();
    });

    // Supprimer une ligne via Event Delegation
    container.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        if (e.target.closest('.remove-row')) {
            const rows = container.querySelectorAll('.ingredient-row');
            if (rows.length > 1) {
                e.target.closest('.ingredient-row').remove();
                updateRowIndices();
            }
        }
    });
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

<!-- Script de Filtrage Dynamique côté Client -->
<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const categoryFilter = document.getElementById('categoryFilter');
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.plat-card');

    categoryFilter.addEventListener('change', function() {
        const selectedCategoryId = this.value;

        cards.forEach(card => {
            const cardCategoryId = card.getAttribute('data-category-id');

            if (selectedCategoryId === 'ALL' || cardCategoryId === selectedCategoryId) {
                card.style.display = 'block';
            } else {
                card.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });
    });
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
